# Skills · S · part 262

824 entries, spectral-database-matching to spectrumworx-engine. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **spectral-database-matching**: Use when when you have MS2 .mzML format data files from untargeted metabolomics or proteomics experiments and need to perform an initial annotation step by matching experimental spectra against known reference libraries (GNPS, HMDB, MassBank) with a defined precursor mass tolerance (e.g., 15 ppm). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/spectral-database-matching) · 4 copies)
- **spectral-database-output-normalization**: Use when you have executed batch spectral searches against two or more domain-specific MASST tools and received heterogeneous output formats (domain-specific HTML trees, JSON objects, TSV match tables) that need to be reconciled into a single normalized schema for downstream aggregation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/spectral-database-output-normalization) · 2 copies)
- **spectral-database-query-execution**: Use when when you have an unknown mass spectrum (query spectrum) and need to search it against a reference database of billions of spectra to find matching or structurally related compounds. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/spectral-database-query-execution) · 2 copies)
- **spectral-database-querying**: Use when you need to programmatically list or filter compounds, NMR or mass spectra, and their metadata from a PeakForest database, e.g. to reproduce a demonstrator compound collection such as the 96-compound set. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/spectral-database-querying))
- **spectral-database-sampling**: Use when building a training/validation/test corpus of pseudo-mixture NMR spectra for a Siamese-style compound identifier, when only a standard-spectrum database and experimental mixture spectra are available. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/spectral-database-sampling))
- **spectral-database-schema-validation**: Use when when you have compiled raw mass spectrometry transition data (precursor m/z, product m/z, intensity, retention time, compound IDs) from experiments or external sources, and need to prepare it for ingestion into the EISA-EXPOSOME R Shiny platform for suspect screening. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/spectral-database-schema-validation) · 2 copies)
- **spectral-database-search-and-scoring**: Use when you have preprocessed mass spectrometry data (peak-picked, baseline-corrected) from DI-MS, ASAP-MS, or LDI-MS instruments and need to identify unknown samples by comparing their spectral fingerprints against a validated reference database of known species or compounds. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/spectral-database-search-and-scoring) · 2 copies)
- **spectral-dataset-curation**: Use when building a cleaned training/reference spectral dataset from a raw GNPS library dump and you must reproduce reproducible filter thresholds (m/z range, minimum peak count) and InChIKey-annotated subsets. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/spectral-dataset-curation))
- **spectral-dataset-partitioning-and-train-test-curation**: Use when when you have a pre-cleaned spectral library (e.g., GNPS, MoNA, or MTBLS1572) with an existing training/test boundary established by prior work (e.g., MSBERT), and you need to report model performance with uncertainty quantification across multiple random partitions. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/spectral-dataset-partitioning-and-train-test-curation) · 2 copies)
- **spectral-dataset-quality-control**: Use when you receive MGF files from an LC-MS/MS run (e.g. PXD034932 serum data) and need to verify dataset completeness or detect outliers and batch effects prior to downstream search. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/spectral-dataset-quality-control))
- **spectral-deconvolution-cosine-similarity**: Use when processing MS/MS spectra from high-dimensional (e.g. LC-IMS-MS/MS) acquisitions where fragments must be attributed to precursor ions and retention time alone is insufficient to resolve overlapping features. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/spectral-deconvolution-cosine-similarity))
- **spectral-deconvolution-integration**: Use when 2D or pseudo-3D NMR spectra contain overlapping cross-peaks requiring quantitative per-peak parameters (amplitude, width, T1/T2 relaxation, concentration) that simple peak picking cannot resolve. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/spectral-deconvolution-integration))
- **spectral-denoising-formula-method**: Use when you have a noisy MS/MS spectrum and need to identify and remove chemical noise ions (as opposed to electronic noise). You have the precursor compound's SMILES string or molecular formula and its adduct type. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/spectral-denoising-formula-method) · 2 copies)
- **spectral-denoising-via-training-data-filtering**: Use when you have untargeted MS2 spectra from environmental or clinical samples that will be used for natural product identification (e.g., linking to BGCs via IOKR or other kernel-based methods), and you have access to a high-quality training library of annotated spectra with known structures. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/spectral-denoising-via-training-data-filtering) · 3 copies)
- **spectral-descriptor-generation-from-massbank**: Use when raw experimental mass spectra (EI-MS or ESI-MS/MS, positive ion mode) must be turned into fixed-length numeric feature vectors — e.g., to train or apply a classifier like BitterMasS on spectral input rather than chemical structures. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/spectral-descriptor-generation-from-massbank))
- **spectral-diagonal-region-masking**: Use when converting MS-DIAL-derived precursor/fragment m/z and intensity pairs into a 2D grayscale fingerprint image and needing to remove the structurally impossible MS2 &gt; MS1 region before CNN training. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/spectral-diagonal-region-masking))
- **spectral-difficulty-stratification**: Use when comparing quantification methods across multiple mixture-spectrum benchmarks and you need to show whether a method's advantage scales with spectral difficulty rather than just averaging scores. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/spectral-difficulty-stratification))
- **spectral-dimension-verification**: Use when after reading an imzML file (continuous or processed format) using readMSIData() in Cardinal, verify the resulting MSImagingExperiment object before performing normalization, baseline reduction, peak-picking, or statistical analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/spectral-dimension-verification) · 2 copies)
- **spectral-dimensionality-reduction**: Use when you have high-resolution tandem MS spectra (in mzML, mzXML, or MGF format) that need to be clustered or searched at scale (millions of spectra). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/spectral-dimensionality-reduction) · 3 copies)
- **spectral-dissimilarity-outlier-detection**: Use when an extract prioritization run includes MS2 spectral data and a MEMO dissimilarity matrix, and you need the SC contribution (0–1) to Inventa's Priority Score highlighting spectrally dissimilar extracts. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/spectral-dissimilarity-outlier-detection))
- **spectral-distance-matrix-averaging-across-individuals**: Use when multiple MS/MS datasets (MGF tandem mass spectra files, e.g. sera tryptic digests) are available per taxon and a species- or group-level phylogenetic tree must be built from pairwise spectral comparisons. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/spectral-distance-matrix-averaging-across-individuals))
- **spectral-distance-matrix-clustering**: Use when you already have a pairwise spectral similarity/distance matrix for untargeted LC-MS/MS features and need to partition features into structural-similarity groups with a data-driven choice of K. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/spectral-distance-matrix-clustering))
- **spectral-distance-matrix-computation**: Use when you have two or more LC-MS/MS datasets (e.g. MGF files with tens of thousands of tandem mass spectra) and need quantitative pairwise spectral distances — e. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/spectral-distance-matrix-computation))
- **spectral-document-representation-handling**: Use when you have per-sample MS2 spectra (in matchms-compatible formats like mzML, mzXML, MGF, or msp) and need to compare metabolomic samples across different LC methods, mass spectrometers, or retention-time regimes—especially when samples are chemodiverse with poor feature overlap or strong RT. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/spectral-document-representation-handling) · 2 copies)
- **spectral-dot-product-similarity-computation**: Use when a large MS/MS training corpus (e.g., ~200,000 downsampled MassIVE-KB-derived PSM spectra) must be reduced to a small, diversity-covering reference subset, and spectra-to-spectra similarity must be quantified classically (normalized dot product) rather than via a learned embedding. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/spectral-dot-product-similarity-computation))
- **spectral-eigendecomposition-of-similarity-matrices**: Use when a cell-to-cell similarity or affinity matrix has been learned (e.g. by SSRE, SSR, MPSSC/SIMLR-style methods) and the cluster number must be estimated from its Laplacian eigenvalue spectrum rather than pre-annotated cell-type counts. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/spectral-eigendecomposition-of-similarity-matrices))
- **spectral-eigenvector-embedding**: Use when you have a binarized cell-by-bin accessibility matrix (e.g., 5-kb bins in a snap file) and need a peak-free low-dimensional embedding of single-cell chromatin profiles for clustering rare populations. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/spectral-eigenvector-embedding))
- **spectral-embedding**: Comprehensive guide to spectral embedding. Master the concepts, implementation, best practices, and real-world applications of spectral embedding in professional environments. ([Sandeeprdy1729/skill_galaxy](https://github.com/Sandeeprdy1729/skill_galaxy/tree/HEAD/skills/ai-ml/spectral-embedding) · ★1)
- **spectral-embedding-dimension-reduction-parameters**: Use when after generating a tile matrix or feature count matrix from single-cell ATAC-seq, RNA-seq, Hi-C, or methylation data, before clustering or UMAP visualization, when you need unsupervised dimension reduction that scales to millions of cells and is agnostic to the underlying data modality. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v1/skills/spectral-embedding-dimension-reduction-parameters))
- **spectral-embedding-distance-clustering**: Use when millions of unlabeled MS/MS spectra (e.g. mzML or MGF peak files) must be grouped into peptide-level clusters without per-spectrum identification, as in spectral library or MassIVE-KB-scale analyses. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/spectral-embedding-distance-clustering))
- **spectral-embedding-extraction-from-neural-networks**: Use when you have a set of preprocessed MS/MS spectra (binned to 10,000 m/z bins with intensities square-root transformed) and a trained MS2DeepScore Siamese neural network model, and you need to compute 200-dimensional spectral embeddings for dimensionality reduction, clustering, or chemical space. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/spectral-embedding-extraction-from-neural-networks) · 3 copies)
- **spectral-embedding-generation**: Use when you have a collection of pre-processed MS/MS spectra (binned, intensity-normalized) and a trained MS2DeepScore base network, and you need to compute structural similarity scores between spectrum pairs or visualize spectra in chemical space via dimensionality reduction (e.g., UMAP). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/spectral-embedding-generation) · 2 copies)
- **spectral-embedding-learner**: Self-learning topological embedding with configurable gamut for optimal spectral gap and fast mixing random walks. ([plurigrid/asi](https://github.com/plurigrid/asi/tree/HEAD/skills/spectral-embedding-learner) · ★72)
- **spectral-embedding-model-training**: Use when you have a large curated MS/MS library (e.g. GNPS-derived AllPositive: 95,320 positive-mode spectra, 77,092 InChIKey-annotated) and need a learned spectral similarity model for library matching or molecular networking beyond plain cosine scoring. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/spectral-embedding-model-training))
- **spectral-embedding-neural-network-training**: Use when retraining or reproducing the GLEAMS Siamese spectral embedder — e.g. ablating precursor/fragment/reference-spectrum feature blocks — from MassIVE-KB PSMs rather than running the pretrained model. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/spectral-embedding-neural-network-training))
- **spectral-embedding-of-normalized-laplacian**: Use when a cell-cell similarity matrix is available and a low-dimensional (e.g., 2D) cell embedding must be initialized before SGD-based refinement, as in EDGE on scRNA-seq data. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/spectral-embedding-of-normalized-laplacian))
- **spectral-embedding-pca-clustering**: Use when you have held-out MS/MS spectra (e.g., GeMS HDF5) and want to verify that pre-trained spectral embeddings organize linearly by molecular structure, or to quantify structural clustering of spectra across differing ionization/fragmentation conditions. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/spectral-embedding-pca-clustering))
- **spectral-embedding-rationality-verification**: Use when after training a transformer-encoder-based mass spectrometry embedding model (e. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/spectral-embedding-rationality-verification) · 2 copies)
- **spectral-embedding-scalability-benchmarking**: Use when you have a large single-cell count matrix (≥10 million cells) in CSR format and need to verify whether the matrix-free spectral embedding in SnapATAC2 achieves its documented linear scaling behavior on your hardware and dataset characteristics. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v1/skills/spectral-embedding-scalability-benchmarking))
- **spectral-embedding-similarity-networking**: Use when you need to organize repository-scale collections of unannotated tandem mass spectra (e.g. the 201 million positive-mode MS/MS spectra in MassIVE GNPS) into a similarity network that enables propagation of annotations from spectral-library nodes to unknowns. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/spectral-embedding-similarity-networking))
- **spectral-embedding-uncertainty-estimation**: Use when MS2DeepScore Tanimoto predictions must be accompanied by a reliability measure, or when only the most confident subset of spectrum-pair scores is acceptable and a precision/retention trade-off is needed. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/spectral-embedding-uncertainty-estimation))
- **spectral-entropy-calculation**: Use when when you have preprocessed MS/MS spectral peak data (m/z and intensity pairs) and need to compute a complexity metric for individual spectra prior to similarity comparisons, or when benchmarking compound identification performance against dot product–based methods. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/spectral-entropy-calculation) · 2 copies)
- **spectral-entropy-calculation**: Use when MS/MS peak arrays ([m/z, intensity] pairs, intensities summing to 1) need a complexity measure or when scoring query-vs-library spectral matches for small-molecule identification, e.g. against NIST20 or MassBank of North America. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/spectral-entropy-calculation))
- **spectral-entropy-quality-assessment**: Use when after feature detection and alignment in untargeted MS data processing, when you need to filter or rank candidate metabolite annotations by spectral quality before committing to xenobiotic metabolite assignments. Use when combining fragmentation similarity scores (e. - **spectral-entropy-similarity-computation**: Use when you need to measure the similarity between two MS/MS spectra as a continuous value that reflects both peak presence/absence and intensity patterns, particularly when comparing noisy versus denoised spectrum variants, or when ranking candidate reference library matches during compound. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/spectral-entropy-similarity-computation) · 2 copies)
- **spectral-entropy-similarity-scoring**: Use when comparing MS/MS spectra (query peaks + precursor m/z against a spectral library) to rank matches with entropy similarity instead of dot-product/cosine, or when benchmarking search speed against LipidIN's EQ module. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/spectral-entropy-similarity-scoring--8aefed36ce7d56fd))
- **spectral-entropy-similarity-scoring**: Use when matching experimental MS/MS spectra against spectral libraries (NIST20, MassBank/MoNA, GNPS), when comparing similarity scoring algorithms for compound identification, or when quantifying noise robustness of spectral similarity scores. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/spectral-entropy-similarity-scoring--59b318cb245101e1))
- **spectral-entropy-similarity-scoring**: Use when scoring PSMs in a proteomic SIP workflow and you need an intensity-distribution-sensitive similarity metric (spectral entropy) alongside MVH/Xcorr/WDP to compare observed vs. theoretical spectra. - **spectral-feature-engineering**: Use when you have molecular structures (SMILES or graph representations) and need to predict or analyze infrared spectral properties using message passing neural networks. - **spectral-feature-extraction**: Use when you have raw mass-spectrometry data (precursor m/z, ionization mode, and fragment m/z–intensity pairs) and need to feed it into a CNN-based metabolite annotation pipeline. - **spectral-feature-extraction-and-annotation**: Use when you have raw LC/MS data in mzML format and need to perform non-targeted screening to discover unknown chemical features without a predefined list of target compounds. - **spectral-library-matching**: Use when you need to validate in silico (CFM-ID) fragment predictions for DNA adducts or other small molecules against experimental tandem HRMS/MS spectra of reference standards before using predicted fragments for annotation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/spectral-library-matching--152f1fcc549360d9))
- **spectral-library-matching**: Use when MS/MS spectra (.mgf) paired with a feature quantification table (.csv) need library annotations, and each matched node's cosine score and matched-peak count must be extracted and compared to reference values. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/spectral-library-matching--c3bacc8572caec82))
- **spectral-library-matching**: Use when you have one or more MS/MS spectra in .mgf format (or USI identifiers) and need to: (1) identify unknowns by searching against domain-curated reference data; (2) assign Level 2 metabolomics annotations via GNPS library matches; (3) aggregate matches across organism lineages (e. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/spectral-library-matching) · 2 copies)
- **spectral-library-matching**: Use when a 1H NMR mixture spectrum must be decomposed and quantified against a library of known component spectra, especially if peaks overlap, are unexpectedly shifted (e.g., different solvent or 36.7 °C vs 22. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/spectral-library-matching--a235177bab475192))
- **spectral-library-matching**: Use when annotating unknown metabolite features from experimental tandem MS/MS peak lists and a set of candidate chemical structures is available for which predicted spectra can be generated. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/spectral-library-matching--0d0e7f9be23e315a))
- **spectral-library-matching**: Use when MS2 or MS3+ spectra from LC-MS lipidomics runs (e.g. Thermo .raw files) must be matched against an in silico lipid spectral library to identify lipids and annotate chromatographic features. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/spectral-library-matching--f9cb86fd6a9f7559))
- **spectral-library-matching**: Use when you have NP3 MS Workflow clean consensus spectra (post-clustering, post-cleaning) and need to assign structural annotations to m/z ions — e. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/spectral-library-matching--a783b0b1b264a20a))
- **spectral-library-matching**: Use when query MS/MS spectra in MSP format must be annotated against a reference spectral library, especially when electronic/chemical noise degrades similarity scores or low-abundance exposome compounds must be recovered. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/spectral-library-matching--2325b90f925587b9))
- **spectral-library-matching**: Use when a GC/EI-MS (70 eV) analyte spectrum fails to match any known reference spectra (NIST, Wiley, or SWGDRUG libraries) and you need to rank potential NPS identities by combined spectral and fingerprint similarity against a synthetic derivative database. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/spectral-library-matching--55241d3be7a468a0))
- **spectral-library-matching**: Use when acquired LC-MS/MS spectra need metabolite annotations (MSI Level 2/3), or when a query MS/MS spectrum must be searched against public metabolomics repositories via tools like GNPS MASST or tissueMASST. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/spectral-library-matching--c9ea083859d5db3b))
- **spectral-library-matching**: Use when query spectra (e.g., NIST 2017 replicates) must be identified against a reference library whose coverage is extended with model-predicted spectra, and ranked-match accuracy (recall@10) is the target metric. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/spectral-library-matching--81a6e04ba6c9285f))
- **spectral-library-matching**: Use when raw or centroided MS/MS spectra must be identified by searching against a spectral library, and score-threshold/FDR behavior of the similarity metric matters. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/spectral-library-matching--e4ae04cd049e123b))
- **spectral-library-matching**: Use when you have MS/MS spectra in MGF form plus a feature table from LC-MS profiling of a natural product extract and need an initial ranked list of candidate structures via spectral similarity against a library like ISDB-DNP or GNPS. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/spectral-library-matching--323d47cecffdb346))
- **spectral-library-matching**: Use when you have aligned deconvolved compounds (AlignID entries from erah's alignComp/recMissComp output) and need to assign metabolite names and match factors before integrating peak areas for downstream statistics. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/spectral-library-matching--270fc7c297baf5bb))
- **spectral-library-matching**: Use when you need to quantify how useful predicted spectra are for database lookup against a non-overlapping reference library, rather than just comparing predicted vs. true spectra directly. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/spectral-library-matching--1031df05e3615df9))
- **spectral-library-matching**: Use when query MS/MS spectra (.mgf from MZmine or USI lists in .csv/.tsv) need library annotation and taxonomic attribution against microbial monoculture data in GNPS/MassIVE, Metabolomics Workbench, Metabolights, and NORMAN. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/spectral-library-matching--5b4423acc23bc3a3))
- **spectral-library-matching**: Use when you have deconvoluted MS/MS peak lists from untargeted LC-MS/MS runs (e.g., HILIC or CSH Q Exactive HF) and need to assign compound identifications at graded MSI confidence levels 1-3. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/spectral-library-matching--c35c7c88958921fa))
- **spectral-library-matching**: Use when you have query MS/MS spectra with precursor m/z to match against a curated library (e.g. GNPS ALL_GNPS.json) and need to compare embedding-based (Spec2Vec) vs peak-matching (cosine) retrieval accuracy. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/spectral-library-matching--599e7745b946f0de))
- **spectral-library-matching**: Use when you have cleaned MGF (or mzML/mzXML/msp/USI) spectra and need all-pairs similarity scores, e.g. cosine or modified cosine, plus matching-peak counts to flag related spectra pairs. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/spectral-library-matching--f9e13d75e017781f))
- **spectral-library-matching**: Use when comparing spectral library search methods (MS2Query, MS2Deepscore, cosine, modified cosine) on MS2 query spectra to establish which achieves the highest true-positive percentage across recall values for exact matches. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/spectral-library-matching--2bf7126f4f4feb10))
- **spectral-library-matching-and-m-z-peak-ranking**: Use when you have raw GC-MS output in CSV format (with Component.RT, Base.Peak.MZ, Component.Area, Compound.Name, Match.Factor, and File.Name columns) and need to systematically rank putative identifications by match quality and exact mass agreement. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/spectral-library-matching-and-m-z-peak-ranking) · 3 copies)
- **spectral-library-matching-and-ranking**: Use when when you have discovered Mass2Motifs from MS2LDA topic modeling and need to automatically annotate them by finding the most structurally similar known spectra in a reference library. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/spectral-library-matching-and-ranking) · 3 copies)
- **spectral-library-matching-and-scoring**: Use when when you have extracted a mean or ROI spectrum from MSI data (via centroid or profile mode conversion) and need to identify the biochemical composition by comparing against curated reference libraries such as LIPID MAPS, HMDB, or a custom metabolite database. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/spectral-library-matching-and-scoring) · 2 copies)
- **spectral-library-matching-and-scoring**: Use when when you have MS/MS fragment spectra (in .mgf format) acquired from unknown metabolite features and need to annotate them against known compounds. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v1/skills/spectral-library-matching-and-scoring))
- **spectral-library-matching-annotation**: Use when you have MS2 spectral data (precursor m/z, retention time, and fragment ion patterns) from UPLC-HRMS analysis of environmental or biological samples and need to assign compound identities by comparing against known reference spectra. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/spectral-library-matching-annotation) · 2 copies)
- **spectral-library-matching-cosine-similarity**: Use when consensus spectra from GC×GC–TOFMS peak picking (e.g. GcDUO $cor_spectra) must be assigned compound identities against an MSP library, with a cosine-similarity cutoff and optional RI window. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/spectral-library-matching-cosine-similarity))
- **spectral-library-matching-evaluation**: Use when you have a trained spectral embedding model (e.g., MSBERT, Spec2Vec) and need to benchmark its library matching accuracy against reference spectra on a test dataset. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/spectral-library-matching-evaluation) · 2 copies)
- **spectral-library-matching-gnps**: Use when you have MS2 product-ion spectra in open formats (.mzML or .mzXML) from public mass spectrometry datasets (e.g., from MassIVE with a valid accession) and need to identify chemical compounds by comparing fragmentation patterns against the GNPS reference spectral library. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/spectral-library-matching-gnps) · 3 copies)
- **spectral-library-matching-isdb-lotus**: Use when MZmine-processed LC-HRMS2 DDA features need structural annotation against a natural-products library, especially when taxonomic metadata (e.g., Open Tree of Life ottIDs) can be exploited to reweight spectral matches. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/spectral-library-matching-isdb-lotus))
- **spectral-library-matching-massbank-nist**: Use when quantifying how many spectra in an MS/MS dataset (e.g., four beer extracts in positive and negative ionization mode) can be identified by library matching, to benchmark novel annotation methods. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/spectral-library-matching-massbank-nist))
- **spectral-library-matching-ripp**: Use when you have tandem mass spectrometry data (LC-MS/MS in MGF, mzXML, mzML, or mzData format) and genomic data from a target organism, and you want to identify RiPPs by matching experimental spectra against a database of predicted post-translationally modified RiPP structures derived from. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/spectral-library-matching-ripp) · 3 copies)
- **spectral-library-matching-validation**: Use when you have GC-MS data preprocessed into a structured spread format and need to confirm that a set of known or suspected compounds are correctly identified in your samples. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/spectral-library-matching-validation) · 3 copies)
- **spectral-library-matching-with-cosine-similarity**: Use when you have a query spectrum and a reduced candidate set of library spectra (via approximate nearest neighbor indexing), and need to search for exact unmodified peptide matches with high confidence before proceeding to variable-mass-shift modification searches. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/spectral-library-matching-with-cosine-similarity) · 2 copies)
- **spectral-library-matching-with-taxonomy**: Use when you have MS/MS spectra (.mgf) and candidate metabolite annotations (with m/z, retention time, chemical identifiers) linked to a known organism or taxon, and you want to rank annotations by both spectral similarity AND biochemical likelihood in that organism's lineage. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/spectral-library-matching-with-taxonomy) · 2 copies)
- **spectral-library-merging-and-deduplication**: Use when when building a comprehensive reference spectral library for metabolomics or chemical identification, you have multiple source libraries in different formats (msp, mgf, NIST binary) and ionization modes (positive/negative MS/MS or EI) that need to be combined into a single. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/spectral-library-merging-and-deduplication) · 4 copies)
- **spectral-library-metadata-annotation**: Use when an .msp mass spectral library file contains records lacking metadata such as SMILES, InChI, InChIKey, CAS numbers, chemical formula, or molecular weight, and the records need post-processing to enrich them. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/spectral-library-metadata-annotation))
- **spectral-library-metadata-assignment**: Use when you have a compiled EI or MS2 library object (read from MSP format via read_lib) and access to NIST ri.dat and USER.DBU files; you need to populate RI values for capillary GC-MS workflows. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/spectral-library-metadata-assignment) · 4 copies)
- **spectral-library-metadata-curation**: Use when building a benchmarking or reference set of experimental MS/MS spectra from public spectral libraries (e.g. GNPS) and library entries need filtering, metadata cleaning, and structural ID standardization. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/spectral-library-metadata-curation))
- **spectral-library-metadata-parsing**: Use when integrating OMSLs such as GNPS, MoNA, MassBank, or MS-Dial-VS17 and you need to extract harmonized InChIKeys per spectrum to quantify identifier overlap, per-library compound counts, and spectral redundancy. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/spectral-library-metadata-parsing))
- **spectral-library-metadata-reorganization**: Use when when ingesting mass spectral libraries (EI or MS2) where SMILES information is embedded in the Comment field rather than in a dedicated SMILES metadata field—particularly common in MoNA GC-MS and LC-MS/MS exports. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/spectral-library-metadata-reorganization) · 3 copies)
- **spectral-library-molecular-networking**: Use when you have deconvolved GC-MS spectra (from overlapping chromatographic peaks) in MGF or mzTab format and want to group chemically related compounds, visualize their similarity relationships, and identify spectral families without prior library matching. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/spectral-library-molecular-networking) · 4 copies)
- **spectral-library-msp-serialization**: Use when after generating in-memory lipid spectra (with m/z, intensity, and metadata such as lipid class, fatty acid composition, and adduct type) when you need to export those spectra as a reusable MSP-format spectral library for downstream identification tasks in Excalibur, Skyline, or NIST MS. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/spectral-library-msp-serialization) · 2 copies)
- **spectral-library-node-identification**: Use when an LC-MS/MS molecular network contains an unassigned node (e.g. an unknown m/z like 328.211) sitting in a molecular family alongside library-hit analogs, and you need to reidentify known compounds or localize the modification between known and unknown analogs. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/spectral-library-node-identification))
- **spectral-library-overlap-quantification**: Use when you must quantify compound-level overlap between a new spectral library and reference open/commercial libraries (GNPS, MassBank EU, MoNA, mzCloud, NIST23) using first-InChIKey-block set differences. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/spectral-library-overlap-quantification))
- **spectral-library-overlap-removal-against-training-set**: Use when preparing an independent evaluation set for a spectral-translation model trained on NIST 20 / synthetic ZINC spectra, to prevent train–test compound leakage before computing Acc@k or Tanimoto metrics. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/spectral-library-overlap-removal-against-training-set))
- **spectral-library-pair-eligibility-filtering**: Use when building an analog-pair benchmark from GNPS MS/MS spectral libraries (e.g., GNPS-MSMLS, NIH-NATURALPRODUCTSLIBRARY_ROUND2_POSITIVE, BERKELEY-LAB) and you need to keep only structurally related pairs with exactly one modification site. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/spectral-library-pair-eligibility-filtering))
- **spectral-library-pair-enumeration**: Use when you need to build a structurally grounded analog benchmark from public MS/MS library entries (spectra + SMILES) for modification-site-localization or analog-search evaluation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/spectral-library-pair-enumeration))
- **spectral-library-pair-retrieval-benchmarking**: Use when you hold MS/MS spectra with SMILES/InChI structural annotations (e.g. a held-out GNPS-derived test set) and must compare similarity measures on retrieving structurally related pairs. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/spectral-library-pair-retrieval-benchmarking))
- **spectral-library-parallel-ingestion**: Use when you have multiple MSP or spectral library files (e.g., one per batch of analytical standards, or organized in a directory structure) that need to be read and merged into a single library object for downstream enrichment (SMILES assignment, RI annotation, write operations). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/spectral-library-parallel-ingestion) · 3 copies)
- **spectral-library-polarity-separation**: Use when you have loaded an MS2 library (from NIST, GNPS, or other sources via read_lib()) that contains both positive and negative ionization modes mixed in a single file, and you need to produce two separate, polarity-specific MSP files for use in MS-DIAL or similar tandem MS analysis software. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/spectral-library-polarity-separation) · 3 copies)
- **spectral-library-query-reference-pairing**: Use when when evaluating a trained spectral embedding model on publicly available datasets (GNPS, MoNA, MTBLS1572, MassBank, or MassSpecGym) and you need to report averaged performance metrics with standard deviation to demonstrate robustness and reproducibility. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/spectral-library-query-reference-pairing) · 2 copies)
- **spectral-library-record-structuring**: Use when you have extracted MS1 and MS2 scans (in mzML/mzXML format) from raw chromatogram files and possess user-provided metadata (retention time, m/z, compound name, molecular weight, annotation fields) that you need to bind together into a queryable spectral library record for local compound. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/spectral-library-record-structuring) · 2 copies)
- **spectral-library-schema-validation**: Use when after harmonizing MS/MS spectra and metadata fields (compound identifiers, adduct annotations, collision energies, instrument types) to a common schema, and before exporting the spectral library to standardized formats (mzML, mzTab, or repository-native format). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/spectral-library-schema-validation) · 2 copies)
- **spectral-library-search**: Use when you have an IDSL.IPA-aligned peak table with individual sample peak lists, a reference MS/MS spectral library (e.g. NIST 2020), and you need to quantify how often IDSL.UFA's MS1 isotope-ranked formula hits agree with MS/MS-confirmed compound formulas. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/spectral-library-search--8faadf2b5526f017))
- **spectral-library-search**: Use when query tandem MS spectra must be matched to candidate molecular structures in a database (e.g., GNPS spectral library, DNP, PubChem, LMSD) to identify small molecules or lipids. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/spectral-library-search--2793a4b174d25d8d))
- **spectral-library-search**: Use when query spectra (e.g., MGF files from HEK293 or iPRG2012) must be searched against a large peptide spectral library while allowing arbitrary modifications within a 500 Da precursor mass window. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/spectral-library-search--7903565b90ccb103))
- **spectral-library-search-annotation**: Use when an experimental MS/MS spectrum (query_spectra.msp with 'peaks' and 'precursor_mz') must be annotated against a reference library (reference_library.msp) and the true precursor molecular formula is unknown, so all formula candidates within mass accuracy must be scored. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/spectral-library-search-annotation))
- **spectral-library-search-execution**: Use when DIA raw acquisitions (.raw/.d/.wiff) must be searched with DIA-NN against a spectral library to produce 1% FDR-filtered peptide/protein identifications and per-file protein counts. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/spectral-library-search-execution))
- **spectral-library-searching**: Use when identifying peptide tandem mass spectra against a large spectral library where modifications may be present and a full pairwise comparison of all library spectra is too slow, e.g. HEK293 human cell line HCD data. - **spectral-peak-extraction**: Use when a JCAMP-DX or mzML spectrum (IR, MS, or 1D 1H/13C NMR) must be reduced to a peak list, and peaks should be picked automatically relative to a default or user-defined intensity threshold. - **spectral-phase-transitions-nn-learning**: Spectral phase transitions and trainability in neural network learning dynamics methodology. Formulates NN training as stochastic evolution of random matrix ensembles, showing BBP (Baik-Ben Arous-Péché) transitions during SGD where isolated eigenvalues detach from random bulk. Derives phase diagram of trainability governed by step size and initial weight variance. Links spectral analysis to representation formation, optimisation hyperparameters, and generalization. Use when: analyzing neural network weight matrix spectra, understanding training dynamics through random matrix theory, BBP trans… ([hiyenwong/ai_collection](https://github.com/hiyenwong/ai_collection/tree/HEAD/collection/skills/other/spectral-phase-transitions-nn-learning) · ★4 · 2 copies)
